----- MODULE CounterDeadlock ----
-EXTENDS Naturals
-VARIABLE x
-Init == x = 0
-Inc == x < 3 /\ x' = x + 1
-Jump == x = 0 /\ x' = 3
-Next == Inc \/ Jump
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_x
-NoJump == ~(x = 0 /\ x' = 3)
-NotThree == x # 3
-====

-# CounterDeadlock
-
-TLC reached 4 states and 3 steps. The chart draws 4 states and 4 arrows.
-1 step that the constraints exclude ends a rule's path, and the chart includes it. TLC checks the rules after such a step, but it does not take the step.
-The chart marks only the deadlock that TLC reports. Under a CONSTRAINT or an ACTION_CONSTRAINT, a state with no step in the chart can still have steps that the constraints leave out, and TLC does not count it as a deadlock.
-
-## NotThree breaks
-The shortest path has 1 step:
-0. 0
-1. Jump: 3
-
-## NoDeadlock breaks
-The shortest path has 3 steps:
-0. 0
-1. Inc: 1
-2. Inc: 2
-3. Inc: 3

-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_<<x, done>>

-# CounterOrder
-
-TLC reached 2 states and 1 steps. The chart draws 3 states and 2 arrows.
-1 state outside the constraints ends a rule's path, and the chart includes it. TLC checks the rules in a state outside the constraints, but it does not explore past it.
-The chart marks only the deadlock that TLC reports. Under a CONSTRAINT or an ACTION_CONSTRAINT, a state with no step in the chart can still have steps that the constraints leave out, and TLC does not count it as a deadlock.

----- MODULE CounterTie ----
-EXTENDS Naturals
-VARIABLE x
-Init == x = 0
-Inc == x' = x + 1
-Bump == x = 0 /\ x' = 3
-Next == Inc \/ Bump
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_x
-Small == x < 5
-NoBump == ~(x = 0 /\ x' = 3)
-NotThree == x # 3
-NotFive == x # 5
-NotOdd == x % 2 = 0
-====
